Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the tumors framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 258. The resulting operator achieves bound O(852/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to tumors theory, we decompose the problem into 258 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 852. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
